# GCP Permissions Reference

AccuKnox's GCP scanner uses a **project-level custom role with 901 read-only permissions** across 41 services. Every permission is a `.list`, `.get`, `.getIamPolicy`, or export/analyze verb, so AccuKnox never reads object contents, table rows, or secret payloads.

Every permission is listed below, grouped by service. Where several permissions serve the same purpose, they share one row. See the [overview](cspm-permissions-overview.md) to compare clouds, or the [GCP prerequisites](cspm-prereq-gcp.md) for setup steps.

!!! note "About the `iam.googleapis.com/*` permissions"
    17 permissions use the fully-qualified `iam.googleapis.com/*` form (Workload Identity Federation and OAuth clients). These are not part of the built-in `roles/viewer` and are included deliberately for full IAM posture visibility.

<div class="iam-tables" markdown="1">

## Access Approval (4)

| Permission | Rationale |
|---|---|
| `accessapproval.requests.get` | **Why we need it:** Reads the detailed configuration of requests to evaluate posture rules against it. Read Access Approval settings and requests.<br>**Findings:** Access Approval Enabled. Asset: access approval settings. |
| `accessapproval.requests.list` | **Why we need it:** Enumerates requests so they appear in the asset inventory. Read Access Approval settings and requests.<br>**Findings:** Access Approval Enabled. Asset: access approval settings. |
| `accessapproval.serviceAccounts.get` | **Why we need it:** Reads the detailed configuration of service accounts to evaluate posture rules against it. Read Access Approval settings and requests.<br>**Findings:** Access Approval Enabled. Asset: access approval settings. |
| `accessapproval.settings.get` | **Why we need it:** Reads the detailed configuration of settings to evaluate posture rules against it. Read Access Approval settings and requests.<br>**Findings:** Access Approval Enabled. Asset: access approval settings. |

## API Gateway (4)

| Permission | Rationale |
|---|---|
| `apigateway.apiconfigs.list` | **Why we need it:** Enumerates apiconfigs so they appear in the asset inventory. List APIs, configs and gateways and read their IAM policy.<br>**Findings:** Gateway public-access IAM checks. Assets: gcp_api_gateway_*. |
| `apigateway.apis.list` | **Why we need it:** Enumerates apis so they appear in the asset inventory. List APIs, configs and gateways and read their IAM policy.<br>**Findings:** Gateway public-access IAM checks. Assets: gcp_api_gateway_*. |
| `apigateway.gateways.getIamPolicy` | **Why we need it:** Reads the IAM policy attached to gateways to detect public or over-permissive access. List APIs, configs and gateways and read their IAM policy.<br>**Findings:** Gateway public-access IAM checks. Assets: gcp_api_gateway_*. |
| `apigateway.gateways.list` | **Why we need it:** Enumerates gateways so they appear in the asset inventory. List APIs, configs and gateways and read their IAM policy.<br>**Findings:** Gateway public-access IAM checks. Assets: gcp_api_gateway_*. |

## API Keys (3)

| Permission | Rationale |
|---|---|
| `apikeys.keys.get` | **Why we need it:** Reads the detailed configuration of keys to evaluate posture rules against it. List API keys and read their restrictions and creation time.<br>**Findings:** API Key Rotation, API Key API Restriction, API Key Application Restriction, API Key Active Services Only. Asset: gcp_apikeys_key. |
| `apikeys.keys.getKeyString` | **Why we need it:** Read-only call against keys used during the scan. List API keys and read their restrictions and creation time.<br>**Findings:** API Key Rotation, API Key API Restriction, API Key Application Restriction, API Key Active Services Only. Asset: gcp_apikeys_key. |
| `apikeys.keys.list` | **Why we need it:** Enumerates keys so they appear in the asset inventory. List API keys and read their restrictions and creation time.<br>**Findings:** API Key Rotation, API Key API Restriction, API Key Application Restriction, API Key Active Services Only. Asset: gcp_apikeys_key. |

| `artifactregistry.versions.list` | **Why we need it:** Enumerates versions so they appear in the asset inventory. List repositories and read their IAM policy and CMEK settings.<br>**Findings:** Repository Public Access, Repository CMK Encryption. Asset: gcp_artifact_registry_repository. |

## BigQuery (4)

| Permission | Rationale |
|---|---|
| `bigquery.datasets.get` | **Why we need it:** Reads the detailed configuration of datasets to evaluate posture rules against it. List datasets and tables and read dataset metadata, access grants and encryption configuration. Table data itself is never read.<br>**Findings:** Datasets CMK Encrypted, Dataset Labels Added, Dataset Public Access, Table Expiration. Assets: gcp_bigquery_dataset, gcp_bigquery_table. |
| `bigquery.datasets.getIamPolicy` | **Why we need it:** Reads the IAM policy attached to datasets to detect public or over-permissive access. List datasets and tables and read dataset metadata, access grants and encryption configuration. Table data itself is never read.<br>**Findings:** Datasets CMK Encrypted, Dataset Labels Added, Dataset Public Access, Table Expiration. Assets: gcp_bigquery_dataset, gcp_bigquery_table. |
| `bigquery.tables.get` | **Why we need it:** Reads the detailed configuration of tables to evaluate posture rules against it. List datasets and tables and read dataset metadata, access grants and encryption configuration. Table data itself is never read.<br>**Findings:** Datasets CMK Encrypted, Dataset Labels Added, Dataset Public Access, Table Expiration. Assets: gcp_bigquery_dataset, gcp_bigquery_table. |
| `bigquery.tables.list` | **Why we need it:** Enumerates tables so they appear in the asset inventory. List datasets and tables and read dataset metadata, access grants and encryption configuration. Table data itself is never read.<br>**Findings:** Datasets CMK Encrypted, Dataset Labels Added, Dataset Public Access, Table Expiration. Assets: gcp_bigquery_dataset, gcp_bigquery_table. |

## Cloud Bigtable (2)

| Permission | Rationale |
|---|---|
| `bigtable.instances.getIamPolicy` | **Why we need it:** Reads the IAM policy attached to instances to detect public or over-permissive access. List instances and read their IAM policy.<br>**Findings:** Instance public-access IAM checks. Asset: gcp_bigtable_instance. |
| `bigtable.instances.list` | **Why we need it:** Enumerates instances so they appear in the asset inventory. List instances and read their IAM policy.<br>**Findings:** Instance public-access IAM checks. Asset: gcp_bigtable_instance. |

## Cloud Billing (4)

| Permission | Rationale |
|---|---|
| `billing.anomalies.get` | **Why we need it:** Reads the detailed configuration of anomalies to evaluate posture rules against it. Read billing account linkage, budgets and cost anomalies for the project.<br>**Findings:** Billing Alerts Enabled, cost-anomaly correlation. |
| `billing.anomalies.list` | **Why we need it:** Enumerates anomalies so they appear in the asset inventory. Read billing account linkage, budgets and cost anomalies for the project.<br>**Findings:** Billing Alerts Enabled, cost-anomaly correlation. |
| `billing.resourceCosts.get` | **Why we need it:** Reads the detailed configuration of resource costs to evaluate posture rules against it. Read billing account linkage, budgets and cost anomalies for the project.<br>**Findings:** Billing Alerts Enabled, cost-anomaly correlation. |
| `billing.resourcebudgets.read` | **Why we need it:** Reads resourcebudgets configuration used by posture rules. Read billing account linkage, budgets and cost anomalies for the project.<br>**Findings:** Billing Alerts Enabled, cost-anomaly correlation. |

| `cloudbuild.connections.get` | **Why we need it:** Reads the detailed configuration of connections to evaluate posture rules against it. List builds and triggers and read their configuration.<br>**Findings:** Build Trigger Permissions, pipeline asset discovery. |
| `cloudbuild.connections.list` | **Why we need it:** Enumerates connections so they appear in the asset inventory. List builds and triggers and read their configuration.<br>**Findings:** Build Trigger Permissions, pipeline asset discovery. |

## Cloud Functions (7)

| Permission | Rationale |
|---|---|
| `cloudfunctions.functions.get` | **Why we need it:** Reads the detailed configuration of functions to evaluate posture rules against it. Assets: gcp_cloudfunctions_function. |
| `cloudfunctions.functions.getIamPolicy` | **Why we need it:** Reads the IAM policy attached to functions to detect public or over-permissive access. List functions and read their configuration, IAM policy and runtime version.<br>**Findings:** Function Public Access, Function Runtime Deprecated, Function Environment Variables checks. Assets: gcp_cloudfunctions_function. |
| `cloudfunctions.functions.list` | **Why we need it:** Enumerates functions so they appear in the asset inventory. List functions and read their configuration, IAM policy and runtime version.<br>**Findings:** Function Public Access, Function Runtime Deprecated, Function Environment Variables checks. Assets: gcp_cloudfunctions_function. |
| `cloudfunctions.functions.sourceCodeGet` | **Why we need it:** Read-only call against functions used during the scan. List functions and read their configuration, IAM policy and runtime version.<br>**Findings:** Function Public Access, Function Runtime Deprecated, Function Environment Variables checks. Assets: gcp_cloudfunctions_function. |
| `cloudfunctions.locations.list` | **Why we need it:** Enumerates locations so they appear in the asset inventory. List functions and read their configuration, IAM policy and runtime version.<br>**Findings:** Function Public Access, Function Runtime Deprecated, Function Environment Variables checks. Assets: gcp_cloudfunctions_function. |
| `cloudfunctions.operations.get` | **Why we need it:** Reads the detailed configuration of operations to evaluate posture rules against it. List functions and read their configuration, IAM policy and runtime version.<br>**Findings:** Function Public Access, Function Runtime Deprecated, Function Environment Variables checks. Assets: gcp_cloudfunctions_function. |
| `cloudfunctions.operations.list` | **Why we need it:** Enumerates operations so they appear in the asset inventory. List functions and read their configuration, IAM policy and runtime version.<br>**Findings:** Function Public Access, Function Runtime Deprecated, Function Environment Variables checks. Assets: gcp_cloudfunctions_function. |

## IAM - service accounts, keys, roles, workload identity (43)

| Permission | Rationale |
|---|---|
| `iam.accesspolicies.get` | **Why we need it:** Reads the detailed configuration of accesspolicies to evaluate posture rules against it. List service accounts and their user-managed keys, read custom roles, and read service-account IAM policies to see who can impersonate them. Also covers Workload Identity Federation pools/providers and OAuth clients (the iam.googleapis.com/* entries), which roles/viewer does not include.<br>**Findings:** Service Account Managed Keys, Service Account Key Rotation, Service Account Admin, Service Account User, Service Account Role, Service Account Separation, Member Admin, Workload Identity Federation trust checks. Assets: gcp_service_account, gcp_service_account_key, gcp_iam_role, workload identity pools/providers. |
| `iam.accesspolicies.list` | **Why we need it:** Enumerates accesspolicies so they appear in the asset inventory. List service accounts and their user-managed keys, read custom roles, and read service-account IAM policies to see who can impersonate them. Also covers Workload Identity Federation pools/providers and OAuth clients (the iam.googleapis.com/* entries), which roles/viewer does not include.<br>**Findings:** Service Account Managed Keys, Service Account Key Rotation, Service Account Admin, Service Account User, Service Account Role, Service Account Separation, Member Admin, Workload Identity Federation trust checks. Assets: gcp_service_account, gcp_service_account_key, gcp_iam_role, workload identity pools/providers. |
| `iam.accesspolicies.searchPolicyBindings` | **Why we need it:** Read-only call against accesspolicies used during the scan. List service accounts and their user-managed keys, read custom roles, and read service-account IAM policies to see who can impersonate them. Also covers Workload Identity Federation pools/providers and OAuth clients (the iam.googleapis.com/* entries), which roles/viewer does not include.<br>**Findings:** Service Account Managed Keys, Service Account Key Rotation, Service Account Admin, Service Account User, Service Account Role, Service Account Separation, Member Admin, Workload Identity Federation trust checks. Assets: gcp_service_account, gcp_service_account_key, gcp_iam_role, workload identity pools/providers. |
| `iam.denypolicies.get` | **Why we need it:** Reads the detailed configuration of denypolicies to evaluate posture rules against it. List service accounts and their user-managed keys, read custom roles, and read service-account IAM policies to see who can impersonate them. ## Cloud Logging (6)

| Permission | Rationale |
|---|---|
| `logging.buckets.get` | **Why we need it:** Reads the detailed configuration of buckets to evaluate posture rules against it. List log sinks, buckets, metrics and exclusions, and read retention settings.<br>**Findings:** Log Sinks Enabled, Audit Logging Enabled, Storage Permissions Logging, VPC Network Logging, VPC Network Route Logging, VPC Firewall Rule Logging, Custom Role Logging, Project Ownership Logging, Audit Configuration Logging. Assets: gcp_logging_sink, gcp_logging_bucket, gcp_logging_metric. |
| `logging.buckets.list` | **Why we need it:** Enumerates buckets so they appear in the asset inventory. List log sinks, buckets, metrics and exclusions, and read retention settings.<br>**Findings:** Log Sinks Enabled, Audit Logging Enabled, Storage Permissions Logging, VPC Network Logging, VPC Network Route Logging, VPC Firewall Rule Logging, Custom Role Logging, Project Ownership Logging, Audit Configuration Logging. Assets: gcp_logging_sink, gcp_logging_bucket, gcp_logging_metric. |
| `logging.logMetrics.list` | **Why we need it:** Enumerates log metrics so they appear in the asset inventory. List log sinks, buckets, metrics and exclusions, and read retention settings.<br>**Findings:** Log Sinks Enabled, Audit Logging Enabled, Storage Permissions Logging, VPC Network Logging, VPC Network Route Logging, VPC Firewall Rule Logging, Custom Role Logging, Project Ownership Logging, Audit Configuration Logging. Assets: gcp_logging_sink, gcp_logging_bucket, gcp_logging_metric. |
| `logging.queries.list` | **Why we need it:** Enumerates queries so they appear in the asset inventory. List log sinks, buckets, metrics and exclusions, and read retention settings.<br>**Findings:** Log Sinks Enabled, Audit Logging Enabled, Storage Permissions Logging, VPC Network Logging, VPC Network Route Logging, VPC Firewall Rule Logging, Custom Role Logging, Project Ownership Logging, Audit Configuration Logging. Assets: gcp_logging_sink, gcp_logging_bucket, gcp_logging_metric. |
| `logging.settings.get` | **Why we need it:** Reads the detailed configuration of settings to evaluate posture rules against it. List log sinks, buckets, metrics and exclusions, and read retention settings.<br>**Findings:** Log Sinks Enabled, Audit Logging Enabled, Storage Permissions Logging, VPC Network Logging, VPC Network Route Logging, VPC Firewall Rule Logging, Custom Role Logging, Project Ownership Logging, Audit Configuration Logging. Assets: gcp_logging_sink, gcp_logging_bucket, gcp_logging_metric. |
| `logging.sinks.list` | **Why we need it:** Enumerates sinks so they appear in the asset inventory. List log sinks, buckets, metrics and exclusions, and read retention settings.<br>**Findings:** Log Sinks Enabled, Audit Logging Enabled, Storage Permissions Logging, VPC Network Logging, VPC Network Route Logging, VPC Firewall Rule Logging, Custom Role Logging, Project Ownership Logging, Audit Configuration Logging. Assets: gcp_logging_sink, gcp_logging_bucket, gcp_logging_metric. |

## Cloud Monitoring (3)

| Permission | Rationale |
|---|---|
| `monitoring.alertPolicies.list` | **Why we need it:** Enumerates alert policies so they appear in the asset inventory. List alert policies, notification channels and metric descriptors.<br>**Findings:** Log Alerts / Alert Policy Enabled checks that pair with the Cloud Logging metrics. |
| `monitoring.monitoredResourceDescriptors.list` | **Why we need it:** Enumerates monitored resource descriptors so they appear in the asset inventory. List alert policies, notification channels and metric descriptors.<br>**Findings:** Log Alerts / Alert Policy Enabled checks that pair with the Cloud Logging metrics. |
| `monitoring.notificationChannels.list` | **Why we need it:** Enumerates notification channels so they appear in the asset inventory. List alert policies, notification channels and metric descriptors.<br>**Findings:** Log Alerts / Alert Policy Enabled checks that pair with the Cloud Logging metrics. |

## Network Connectivity Center (2)

| Permission | Rationale |
|---|---|
| `networkconnectivity.internalRanges.get` | **Why we need it:** Reads the detailed configuration of internal ranges to evaluate posture rules against it. List hubs and spokes.<br>**Findings:** Hub/spoke asset discovery. |
| `networkconnectivity.internalRanges.list` | **Why we need it:** Enumerates internal ranges so they appear in the asset inventory. List hubs and spokes.<br>**Findings:** Hub/spoke asset discovery. |

## Network Management (2)

| Permission | Rationale |
|---|---|
| `networkmanagement.connectivitytests.get` | **Why we need it:** Reads the detailed configuration of connectivitytests to evaluate posture rules against it. List connectivity tests and VPC-SC configuration.<br>**Findings:** Reachability validation supporting Internet Exposure. |
| `networkmanagement.connectivitytests.list` | **Why we need it:** Enumerates connectivitytests so they appear in the asset inventory. List connectivity tests and VPC-SC configuration.<br>**Findings:** Reachability validation supporting Internet Exposure. |

## Network Security (2)

| Permission | Rationale |
|---|---|
| `networksecurity.dnsThreatDetectors.get` | **Why we need it:** Reads the detailed configuration of dns threat detectors to evaluate posture rules against it. List security policies and address groups.<br>**Findings:** Security policy asset discovery and Cloud Armor coverage checks. |
| `networksecurity.dnsThreatDetectors.list` | **Why we need it:** Enumerates dns threat detectors so they appear in the asset inventory. List security policies and address groups.<br>**Findings:** Security policy asset discovery and Cloud Armor coverage checks. |

## Organization Policy (3)

| Permission | Rationale |
|---|---|
| `orgpolicy.constraints.list` | **Why we need it:** Enumerates constraints so they appear in the asset inventory. Read effective org-policy constraints as they apply to the project.<br>**Findings:** Org Policy constraint checks (external IP, SA key creation, shielded VM, uniform bucket access). |
| `orgpolicy.policies.list` | **Why we need it:** Enumerates policies so they appear in the asset inventory. Read effective org-policy constraints as they apply to the project.<br>**Findings:** Org Policy constraint checks (external IP, SA key creation, shielded VM, uniform bucket access). |
| `orgpolicy.policy.get` | **Why we need it:** Reads the detailed configuration of policy to evaluate posture rules against it. Read effective org-policy constraints as they apply to the project.<br>**Findings:** Org Policy constraint checks (external IP, SA key creation, shielded VM, uniform bucket access). |

| Permission | Rationale |

## Secret Manager (5)

| Permission | Rationale |
|---|---|
| `secretmanager.secrets.get` | **Why we need it:** Reads the detailed configuration of secrets to evaluate posture rules against it. List secrets and versions and read secret metadata and IAM policy. Metadata only - secret payloads are never read.<br>**Findings:** Secret Rotation Enabled, Secret Access Restricted, Secret CMK Encryption. Assets: gcp_secret_manager_secret. |
| `secretmanager.secrets.getIamPolicy` | **Why we need it:** Reads the IAM policy attached to secrets to detect public or over-permissive access. List secrets and versions and read secret metadata and IAM policy. Metadata only - secret payloads are never read.<br>**Findings:** Secret Rotation Enabled, Secret Access Restricted, Secret CMK Encryption. Assets: gcp_secret_manager_secret. |
| `secretmanager.secrets.list` | **Why we need it:** Enumerates secrets so they appear in the asset inventory. List secrets and versions and read secret metadata and IAM policy. Metadata only - secret payloads are never read.<br>**Findings:** Secret Rotation Enabled, Secret Access Restricted, Secret CMK Encryption. Assets: gcp_secret_manager_secret. |
| `secretmanager.versions.get` | **Why we need it:** Reads the detailed configuration of versions to evaluate posture rules against it. List secrets and versions and read secret metadata and IAM policy. Metadata only - secret payloads are never read.<br>**Findings:** Secret Rotation Enabled, Secret Access Restricted, Secret CMK Encryption. Assets: gcp_secret_manager_secret. |
| `secretmanager.versions.list` | **Why we need it:** Enumerates versions so they appear in the asset inventory. List secrets and versions and read secret metadata and IAM policy. Metadata only - secret payloads are never read.<br>**Findings:** Secret Rotation Enabled, Secret Access Restricted, Secret CMK Encryption. Assets: gcp_secret_manager_secret. |

| `servicedirectory.namespaces.list` | **Why we need it:** Enumerates namespaces so they appear in the asset inventory. List namespaces, services and endpoints and read their IAM policy.<br>**Findings:** Asset discovery for gcp_service_directory_* plus public-access IAM checks. |
| `servicedirectory.services.get` | **Why we need it:** Reads the detailed configuration of services to evaluate posture rules against it. List namespaces, services and endpoints and read their IAM policy.<br>**Findings:** Asset discovery for gcp_service_directory_* plus public-access IAM checks. |
| `servicedirectory.services.list` | **Why we need it:** Enumerates services so they appear in the asset inventory. List namespaces, services and endpoints and read their IAM policy.<br>**Findings:** Asset discovery for gcp_service_directory_* plus public-access IAM checks. |

## Cloud Storage (4)

| Permission | Rationale |
|---|---|
| `storage.buckets.get` | **Why we need it:** Reads the detailed configuration of buckets to evaluate posture rules against it. List buckets and read their IAM policy, ACLs and configuration. This is the clearest gap in roles/viewer - Storage uses a separate permission bundle, so Viewer alone cannot do bucket exposure checks.<br>**Findings:** Bucket All Users Policy, Bucket Uniform Access, Bucket Versioning, Bucket Logging, Bucket CMK Encryption. Asset: gcp_storage_bucket. |
| `storage.buckets.getIamPolicy` | **Why we need it:** Reads the IAM policy attached to buckets to detect public or over-permissive access. List buckets and read their IAM policy, ACLs and configuration. This is the clearest gap in roles/viewer - Storage uses a separate permission bundle, so Viewer alone cannot do bucket exposure checks.<br>**Findings:** Bucket All Users Policy, Bucket Uniform Access, Bucket Versioning, Bucket Logging, Bucket CMK Encryption. Asset: gcp_storage_bucket. |
| `storage.buckets.list` | **Why we need it:** Enumerates buckets so they appear in the asset inventory. List buckets and read their IAM policy, ACLs and configuration. This is the clearest gap in roles/viewer - Storage uses a separate permission bundle, so Viewer alone cannot do bucket exposure checks.<br>**Findings:** Bucket All Users Policy, Bucket Uniform Access, Bucket Versioning, Bucket Logging, Bucket CMK Encryption. Asset: gcp_storage_bucket. |
| `storage.objects.list` | **Why we need it:** Enumerates objects so they appear in the asset inventory. List buckets and read their IAM policy, ACLs and configuration. This is the clearest gap in roles/viewer - Storage uses a separate permission bundle, so Viewer alone cannot do bucket exposure checks.<br>**Findings:** Bucket All Users Policy, Bucket Uniform Access, Bucket Versioning, Bucket Logging, Bucket CMK Encryption. Asset: gcp_storage_bucket. |
